Module
TensorPackingStatement
:= Struct Definition tensor_packing_statement : Prop. End
TensorPackingStatement.tensor_packing_statement =
forall R : reals.Real.type,
@ex (reals.Real.sort R)
  (fun C : reals.Real.sort R =>
   and
     (is_true
        (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
           (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
           (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
              (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R)))
           C))
     (forall (n1 n2 m d L r : nat) (eps eta : reals.Real.sort R)
        (x : forall _ : fintype.ordinal m,
             matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) (S O) n1)
        (y : forall _ : fintype.ordinal m,
             matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) (S O) n2)
        (Gx
         Gy : forall (_ : fintype.ordinal m) (_ : fintype.ordinal m), bool)
        (A : forall _ : fintype.ordinal r,
             matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) n1 n2)
        (_ : is_true
               (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
                  (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
                  (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
                     (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule
                           R)))
                  eps))
        (_ : is_true
               (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Order_Preorder R) eps
                  (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                     (numdomain.Num.NumDomain.Exports.join_Num_NumDomain_between_Order_Preorder_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
                        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_NumDomain R)))))
        (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S O) d))
        (_ : is_true
               (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
                  (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
                  (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
                     (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule
                           R)))
                  eta))
        (_ : is_true
               (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
                  (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Order_Preorder R) eta
                  (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
                     (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.exp
                        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                           R)
                        eps (S (S O)))
                     (@divalg.GRing.inv
                        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)
                        (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                              (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                                    R)))
                           (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                              (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                                    R)))
                           (S

SKGapDefinitions.spin =
fun (R : reals.Real.type) (b : bool) =>
match
  b
  return
    (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.sort
       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
with
| @true =>
    rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
      (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
         (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))
| @false =>
    @nmodule.Algebra.opp
      (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.GRing_PzRing__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
         (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))
      (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
         (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
            (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
end
     : forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : bool), reals.Real.sort R

Arguments SKGapDefinitions.spin R b%_bool_scope

PerceptronSignDefinitions
:= Struct
     Definition spin_col :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (N : nat)
         (_ : @finfun.finfun_of
                (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite N)
                (fun _ : fintype.ordinal N => bool)
                (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal N, bool))),
       matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) N (S O).
     Definition perceptron_pressure :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (M N : nat)
         (_ : forall _ : reals.Real.sort R, reals.Real.sort R)
         (_ : matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) M N),
       reals.Real.sort R.
     Definition gauss_pressure :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : nat) (_ : nat)
         (_ : forall _ : reals.Real.sort R, reals.Real.sort R),
       reals.Real.sort R.
     Definition sign_pressure :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : nat) (_ : nat)
         (_ : forall _ : reals.Real.sort R, reals.Real.sort R),
       reals.Real.sort R.
     Definition bounded_C3 :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type)
         (_ : forall _ : reals.Real.sort R, reals.Real.sort R)
         (_ : reals.Real.sort R) (_ : reals.Real.sort R)
         (_ : reals.Real.sort R) (_ : reals.Real.sort R),
       Prop.
   End
PerceptronSignDefinitions.spin_col =
fun (R : reals.Real.type) (N : nat)
  (x : @finfun.finfun_of
         (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite N)
         (fun _ : fintype.ordinal N => bool)
         (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal N, bool))) =>
@matrix.matrix_of_fun.body
  (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.sort
     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
  N (S O) matrix.matrix_key
  (fun (i : fintype.ordinal N) (_ : fintype.ordinal (S O)) =>
   match
     @finfun.fun_of_fin
       (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite N)
       (fun _ : fintype.ordinal N => bool)
       (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal N, bool)) x i
     return
       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.sort
          (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
             (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
   with
   | @true =>
       rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
         (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
            (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))
   | @false =>
       @nmodule.Algebra.opp
         (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.GRing_PzRing__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule
            (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R))
         (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
            (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
               (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzRing R)))
   end)
     : forall (R : reals.Real.type) [N : nat]
         (_ : @finfun.finfun_of
                (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite N)
                (fun _ : fintype.ordinal N => bool)
                (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal N, bool))),
       matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) N (S O)

Arguments PerceptronSignDefinitions.spin_col R [N]%_nat_scope x
PerceptronSignDefinitions.perceptron_pressure =
fun (R : reals.Real.type) (M N : nat)
  (f : forall _ : reals.Real.sort R, reals.Real.sort R)
  (G : matrix.matrix (reals.Real.sort R) M N) =>
@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
  (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R))
  (@divalg.GRing.inv (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)
     (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
           (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)))
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
           (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)))
        N))
  (@exp.ln R
     (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
        (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R))
        (@divalg.GRing.inv
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)
           (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.exp
              (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing

SatVarianceDefinitions
:= Struct
     Definition proper_clause :
       forall (n _ : nat)
         (_ : @finfun.finfun_of
                (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                (fun _ : fintype.ordinal n => option bool)
                (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal n, option bool))),
       bool.
     Definition clause :
       forall (_ : nat) (_ : nat), Type@{ssrbool.predArgType.u0}.
     Definition clause_sat :
       forall (n k : nat)
         (_ : @finfun.finfun_of
                (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                (fun _ : fintype.ordinal n => bool)
                (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal n, bool)))
         (_ : clause n k),
       bool.
     Definition prefix_sat :
       forall (n k M : nat) (_ : tuple.tuple_of M (clause n k)) (_ : nat),
       bool.
     Definition sat_cap : forall (_ : nat) (_ : nat), nat.
     Definition sat_prefix_len :
       forall (n k M : nat) (_ : tuple.tuple_of M (clause n k)), nat.
     Definition sat_mean :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (n k M : nat)
         (_ : forall _ : tuple.tuple_of M (clause n k), reals.Real.sort R),
       reals.Real.sort R.
     Definition sat_var :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (n k M : nat)
         (_ : forall _ : tuple.tuple_of M (clause n k), reals.Real.sort R),
       reals.Real.sort R.
     Definition sat_capped_var :
       forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : nat) (_ : nat), reals.Real.sort R.
   End

         (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)

                                       option bool)))
                                (@SatVarianceDefinitions.proper_clause n k)))))
                    c)
                 i)
              (@Some bool
                 (@finfun.fun_of_fin
                    (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                    (fun _ : fintype.ordinal n => bool)
                    (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal n, bool))
                    sigma i))))
        i))
     : forall [n k : nat]
         (_ : @finfun.finfun_of
                (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                (fun _ : fintype.ordinal n => bool)
                (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal n, bool)))
         (_ : SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k),
       bool

SatVarianceDefinitions.prefix_sat =

  (m : nat) =>
negb
  (@fintype.FiniteQuant.quant0b
     (finfun.finfun_finfun_of__canonical__fintype_Finite
        (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
        fintype.Datatypes_bool__canonical__fintype_Finite)
     (fun
        sigma : @finfun.finfun_of
                  (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                  (fun _ : fintype.ordinal n => bool)
                  (ssreflect.Phant (forall _ : fintype.ordinal n, bool)) =>
      @fintype.FiniteQuant.ex
        (finfun.finfun_finfun_of__canonical__fintype_Finite
           (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
           fintype.Datatypes_bool__canonical__fintype_Finite)
        (fintype.FiniteQuant.Quantified
           (@fintype.FiniteQuant.quant0b
              (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite M)
              (fun i : fintype.ordinal M =>
               @fintype.FiniteQuant.all
                 (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite M)
                 (fintype.FiniteQuant.Quantified
                    (implb (ssrnat.leq (S (@fintype.nat_of_ord M i)) m)
                       (@SatVarianceDefinitions.clause_sat n k sigma
                          (@tuple.tnth M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k)
                             t i))))
                 i)))
        sigma))
     : forall [n k M : nat]
         (_ : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k)) 
         (_ : nat),
       bool

SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_mean =
fun (R : reals.Real.type) (n k M : nat)
  (F : forall _ : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k),
       reals.Real.sort R) =>
@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
  (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R))
  (@divalg.GRing.inv (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)
     (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
           (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)))
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
           (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing R)))
        (@fintype.card.body
           (tuple.tuple_tuple_of__canonical__fintype_Finite M
              (@fintype.Specif_sig__canonical__fintype_Finite
                 (finfun.finfun_finfun_of__canonical__fintype_Finite
                    (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                    (fintype.Datatypes_option__canonical__fintype_Finite
                       fintype.Datatypes_bool__canonical__fintype_Finite))
                 (@SatVarianceDefinitions.proper_clause n k)))
           (@ssrbool.mem
              (tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k))
              (ssrbool.predPredType
                 (tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k)))
              (@ssrbool.PredOfSimpl.coerce
                 (tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k))
                 (ssrbool.pred_of_argType
                    (tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k)
                     :
                     ssrbool.predArgType)))))))
  (@bigop.bigop.body
     (nmodule.Algebra.BaseAddUMagma.sort
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma R))
     (fintype.Finite.sort
        (tuple.tuple_tuple_of__canonical__fintype_Finite M
           (@fintype.Specif_sig__canonical__fintype_Finite
              (finfun.finfun_finfun_of__canonical__fintype_Finite
                 (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                 (fintype.Datatypes_option__canonical__fintype_Finite
                    fintype.Datatypes_bool__canonical__fintype_Finite))
              (@SatVarianceDefinitions.proper_clause n k))))
     (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma R))
     (bigop.index_enum
        (tuple.tuple_tuple_of__canonical__fintype_Finite M
           (@fintype.Specif_sig__canonical__fintype_Finite
              (finfun.finfun_finfun_of__canonical__fintype_Finite
                 (fintype.fintype_ordinal__canonical__fintype_Finite n)
                 (fintype.Datatypes_option__canonical__fintype_Finite
                    fintype.Datatypes_bool__canonical__fintype_Finite))
              (@SatVarianceDefinitions.proper_clause n k))))
     (fun t : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k) =>
      @bigop.BigBody
        (nmodule.Algebra.BaseAddMagma.sort
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma R))
        (tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k)) t
        (@nmodule.Algebra.add
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma R))
        true (F t)))
     : forall [R : reals.Real.type] [n k M : nat]
         (_ : forall _ : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k),
              reals.Real.sort R),
       reals.Real.sort R

SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_var =
fun (R : reals.Real.type) (n k M : nat)
  (F : forall _ : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k),
       reals.Real.sort R) =>
@SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_mean R n k M
  (fun t : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k) =>
   @rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.exp
     (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R)
     (@nmodule.Algebra.add
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma R) 
        (F t)
        (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseZmodule R)
           (@SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_mean R n k M F)))
     (S (S O)))
     : forall [R : reals.Real.type] [n k M : nat]
         (_ : forall _ : tuple.tuple_of M (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k),
              reals.Real.sort R),
       reals.Real.sort R

  F%_function_scope
SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_capped_var =

@SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_var R n k (SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_cap k n)
  (fun
     t : tuple.tuple_of (SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_cap k n)
           (SatVarianceDefinitions.clause n k) =>
   @nmodule.Algebra.natmul
     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
        (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
     (@SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_prefix_len n k
        (SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_cap k n) t))
     : forall (R : reals.Real.type) (_ : nat) (_ : nat), reals.Real.sort R

:= Struct Definition sat_variance_statement : Prop. End
SatVarianceStatement.sat_variance_statement =
forall (R : reals.Real.type) (k : nat)
  (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S (S (S O))) k)),
@ex (reals.Real.sort R)
  (fun C : reals.Real.sort R =>
   and
     (is_true
        (@preorder.Order.lt orderedzmod.ring_display
           (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.join_Num_POrderNmodule_between_Algebra_BaseAddUMagma_and_Order_Preorder
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R))
           (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
              (orderedzmod.Num.POrderNmodule.Exports.Num_POrderNmodule__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                 (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Num_POrderNmodule R)))
           C))
     (forall (n : nat) (_ : is_true (ssrnat.leq (S k) n)),
      is_true
        (@preorder.Order.le orderedzmod.ring_display
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Order_Preorder R)
           (SatVarianceDefinitions.sat_capped_var R n k)
           (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
              (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R) C
              (@exp.powR R
                 (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                    (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                       (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
                    (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                       (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_PzSemiRing R))
                    n)
                 (@nmodule.Algebra.add
                    (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma
                       (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                             R)))
                    (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                       (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                             R)))
                    (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
                       (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                             R))
                       (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                          (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                             (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                                   R)))
                          (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                             (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                                (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                                   R)))
                          (S (S O)))
                       (@divalg.GRing.inv
                          (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__GRing_UnitRing
                             R)
                          (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                             (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma

                                      R)))
                             k)))))))))
     : Prop
